# Stripe connection and teacher Home, 2026-09-27

## Verified connection state

- A read-only call using the locally stored `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY` returned HTTP 200 from `GET /v1/account` for `acct_1PJnXRIULVCj6rqq` and HTTP 200 from `GET /v1/payment_intents?limit=1`. No key or payment data was copied into this report.
- The live Stripe event destination points to LSAT U's `booking-commerce-webhook`. A completed Checkout event returned 200 on 2026-09-23. A completed Checkout event on 2026-09-21 had a 500 attempt and was marked recovered after retry. The precise exception behind that 500 still needs a Lovable Cloud log lookup.
- The Codex Stripe connector independently requires reauthentication. This does not invalidate the server API key or existing Stripe webhook.
- The current webhook handles booking Checkout completion/expiration and refunds. It is not a complete transaction feed. The teacher's `/classroom` Home is still the student Home.

## Fastest implementation path

1. Inspect the 2026-09-21 Lovable Cloud function error and fix its cause. Keep existing destination in place while adding coverage.
2. Create a dedicated, signed Stripe event ingest with an append-only event inbox, event-ID deduplication, processing status, and an exception queue. Add only the Stripe events needed for the actual payment channels. Keep the existing booking processing intact until the new ledger is reconciled.
3. Normalize Checkout Sessions, PaymentIntents, invoices, charges, refunds, and disputes to one payment activity record per underlying transaction. Store Stripe object IDs, UTC timestamps, amount/currency, customer, payment status, and provenance. Display failed and pending events as such; do not label them paid.
4. Use server-side Stripe API reads and cursor pagination to backfill the 3 months before activation. Read historical activity first; do not automatically grant old sessions or access. Reconcile the count and totals against Stripe and flag missing/ambiguous records.
5. Match future payments using an internal student ID in Checkout `client_reference_id` or metadata, then Stripe customer ID, with verified email as a fallback. Put ambiguous payments in the teacher exception queue for review.
6. Route teacher/admin Home to a teacher dashboard showing new payments, bookings, package balances, exceptions, webhook health, and searchable payment history. Keep the student study Home for students.
7. Test in Stripe sandbox, deploy through Lovable Cloud, add the required events to the existing live Stripe destination, then verify a new payment, refund, replay, duplicate event, and 3-month reconciliation in production.

## Stripe-side configuration

- The existing webhook signing secret and server API key are present in configuration; verify their Lovable Cloud secret values without exposing them.
- Create a restricted **read** key for the activity/backfill reader with only the resources it needs. Keep any write authority in a separate server-side key. The webhook signing secret is separate from API keys.
- Add the new event types to the existing live destination only when its receiver is ready. Stripe may deliver out of order and retry failed deliveries, so ingestion must be idempotent.
- The Codex Stripe connector can be reauthenticated separately to make administrative inspection easier. It is not required for LSAT U runtime payments.

## Current blockers and decisions

- The old decision that booking passes are the sole source of sessions owed conflicts with automatic package entitlement grants. Supersede it explicitly before enabling grants.
- Decide whether unmatched payers and unrelated Stripe payments appear only in the teacher ledger or also receive student access. Default: show every transaction, grant nothing without a confirmed product and student match.
- Lovable Cloud deployment requires confirmation of that route. Any Lovable action that may spend credits requires a separate confirmation immediately before the action.

## Stripe documentation

- [Webhooks](https://docs.stripe.com/webhooks)
- [Event types](https://docs.stripe.com/api/events/types)
- [API keys and webhook secrets](https://docs.stripe.com/keys)
- [Restricted API keys](https://docs.stripe.com/keys/restricted-api-keys)
- [PaymentIntent listing and pagination](https://docs.stripe.com/api/payment_intents/list)
- [Checkout Sessions](https://docs.stripe.com/api/checkout/sessions)
- [Refund listing](https://docs.stripe.com/api/refunds/list)

async function handleStripe(supabase: any, raw: string, signature: string | null) {
  const stripeSecret = requiredEnv("STRIPE_SECRET_KEY");
  if (!signature) return json({ error: "invalid_signature" }, 400);
  const stripe = new Stripe(stripeSecret, {
    apiVersion: "2025-08-27.basil",
    httpClient: Stripe.createFetchHttpClient(),
  });
  let event: Stripe.Event;
  try {
    event = await stripe.webhooks.constructEventAsync(
      raw,
      signature,
      requiredEnv("STRIPE_BOOKING_WEBHOOK_SECRET"),
      300,
    );
  } catch (_) {
    return json({ error: "invalid_signature" }, 400);
  }

  if (event.type.startsWith("refund.")) {
    const refund = event.data.object as Stripe.Refund;
    if (event.livemode !== expectedLivemode()) return json({ error: "livemode_mismatch" }, 400);
    const bookingId = String(refund.metadata?.booking_id || "");
    if (!bookingId || !refund.id || !refund.status) return json({ error: "invalid_refund" }, 400);
    const { error } = await supabase.rpc("record_single_session_refund_status", {
      _booking_id: bookingId,
      _stripe_refund_id: refund.id,
      _provider_status: refund.status,
    });
    if (error) throw error;
    return json({ received: true });
  }

  if (event.type === "checkout.session.expired") {
    const session = event.data.object;
    if (session.metadata?.product !== "single_session") return json({ received: true, ignored: "not_single_session" });
    if (session.livemode !== expectedLivemode()) return json({ error: "livemode_mismatch" }, 400);
    const bookingId = String(session.metadata?.booking_id || "");
    if (!bookingId || !session.id) return json({ error: "invalid_session" }, 400);
    const { error } = await supabase.rpc("expire_single_session_checkout", {
      _booking_id: bookingId,
      _stripe_checkout_session_id: session.id,
      _reason: "stripe_checkout_expired",
    });
    if (error && error.code !== "P0409") throw error;
    return json({ received: true });
  }

  if (event.type !== "checkout.session.completed") return json({ received: true });
  const session = event.data.object;

  // A payment never changes access. Access is Joshua's decision, made by tier
  // on the People page, and it never changes by itself. What a payment does is
  // tell him, so a paying student is not left waiting unnoticed in the queue.
  if (session.payment_status === "paid") {
    try {
      await notifyAdminsOfPayment(supabase, {
        email: session.customer_details?.email ?? session.customer_email ?? null,
        name: session.customer_details?.name ?? null,
        amountCents: typeof session.amount_total === "number" ? session.amount_total : null,
        currency: session.currency ?? null,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      // A notice is a convenience on top of the payment record. Failing to
      // write it must never make Stripe retry a payment that was recorded.
      console.error("payment notice failed", { event: event.id, err });
    }
  }

  if (session.metadata?.product === "single_session") {
    const bookingId = String(session.metadata?.booking_id || "");
    if (!bookingId) return json({ error: "invalid_product" }, 400);
    if (!session.id || typeof session.payment_intent !== "string") return json({ error: "missing_payment_facts" }, 400);
    const { data: booking, error: bookingError } = await supabase.from("single_session_bookings")
      .select("id,status,start_at,end_at,gcal_event_id,event_slug,email,quoted_amount_cents,quoted_currency")
      .eq("id", bookingId).maybeSingle();
    if (bookingError) throw bookingError;
    if (!booking) return json({ error: "unknown_booking" }, 400);
    const { data: bookingEvent, error: eventError } = await supabase.from("booking_event_types")
      .select("price_cents,currency")
    });
    if (error) throw error;
    // Keep the Stripe response fast: the nudge runs after the response is sent
    // when the runtime supports it, and is otherwise best-effort.
    const nudge = kickOutboxWorker();
    const waitUntil = (globalThis as any)?.EdgeRuntime?.waitUntil;
    if (typeof waitUntil === "function") waitUntil.call((globalThis as any).EdgeRuntime, nudge);
    else void nudge;
    return json({ received: true, booking_id: result?.booking_id, status: result?.status });
  }

  // Existing package branch, now protected by immutable payment validation.
  if (session.mode !== "payment" || session.payment_status !== "paid") return json({ error: "payment_not_complete" }, 400);
  if (session.livemode !== expectedLivemode()) return json({ error: "livemode_mismatch" }, 400);
  const paymentLink = typeof session.payment_link === "string" ? session.payment_link : session.payment_link?.id;
  if (!paymentLink) return json({ received: true, ignored: "not_booking_commerce" });
  const { data: offer, error: offerError } = await supabase.from("booking_offers")
    .select("id,credits").eq("provider", "stripe").eq("provider_ref", paymentLink).eq("active", true).maybeSingle();
  if (offerError) throw offerError;
  if (!offer) return json({ received: true, ignored: "unknown_payment_link" });
  const expected = await expectedPaymentLinkFacts(stripeSecret, paymentLink);
  if (session.amount_total !== expected.amount) return json({ error: "amount_mismatch" }, 400);
  if (String(session.currency || "").toLowerCase() !== expected.currency) return json({ error: "currency_mismatch" }, 400);
  const email = session.customer_details?.email ?? session.customer_email ?? null;
  const student = await matchStudent(supabase, email, null);
  if (!student) {
    const { error } = await supabase.from("booking_commerce_events").upsert({
      provider: "stripe",
      provider_event_id: event.id,
      event_type: "unmatched_paid",
      offer_id: offer.id,
      email,
      amount_cents: session.amount_total,
      currency: session.currency,
      payload: { checkout_session_id: session.id, payment_link: paymentLink, payment_status: session.payment_status, livemode: session.livemode },
    }, { onConflict: "provider,provider_event_id,event_type" });
    if (error) throw error;
    return json({ received: true, match: "pending" });
  }
  const { error } = await supabase.rpc("apply_booking_package_payment", {
    _student_id: student.id,
    _offer_id: offer.id,
    _provider_event_id: event.id,
    _email: email,
  });
  if (error) throw error;
  return json({ received: true, student_id: student.id });
}

Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method !== "POST") return json({ error: "method_not_allowed" }, 405);
  try {
    const raw = await readRaw(req);
    const provider = new URL(req.url).searchParams.get("provider");
    const supabase = createClient(requiredEnv("SUPABASE_URL"), requiredEnv("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"));
    if (provider === "stripe") return await handleStripe(supabase, raw, req.headers.get("stripe-signature"));

    if (provider === "calcom") {
      if (!await verifyCal(raw, req.headers.get("x-cal-signature-256"))) return json({ error: "invalid_signature" }, 400);
      const event = JSON.parse(raw);
      const payload = event.payload ?? event;
    if (error instanceof Error && error.message === "payload_too_large") return json({ error: "payload_too_large" }, 413);
    console.error("booking-commerce-webhook", { error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) });
    return json({ error: "provider_processing_failed" }, 500);
  }
});


/** One notification per admin: someone paid, decide their tier. */
async function notifyAdminsOfPayment(
  supabase: { from: (t: string) => any },
  payment: { email: string | null; name: string | null; amountCents: number | null; currency: string | null },
) {
  const { data: admins, error } = await supabase.from("user_roles").select("user_id").eq("role", "admin");
  if (error) throw error;
  const who = payment.name || payment.email || "Someone";
  const amount =
    payment.amountCents !== null
      ? ` ${(payment.amountCents / 100).toFixed(2)} ${(payment.currency ?? "").toUpperCase()}`.trimEnd()
      : "";
  const rows = (admins ?? []).map((a: { user_id: string }) => ({
    user_id: a.user_id,
    type: "payment_needs_tier",
    title: "Payment received",
    body: `${who}${payment.email && payment.name ? ` (${payment.email})` : ""} paid${amount}. Their access has not changed. Set their tier if they need more.`,
    link: "/teacher/students",
  }));
  if (rows.length === 0) return;
  const { error: insertError } = await supabase.from("notifications").insert(rows);
  if (insertError) throw insertError;
}

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.apply_booking_package_payment(
  _student_id uuid,
  _offer_id text,
  _provider_event_id text,
  _email text DEFAULT NULL
) RETURNS public.booking_passes
LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$
DECLARE
  _offer public.booking_offers%ROWTYPE;
  _pass public.booking_passes%ROWTYPE;
BEGIN
  SELECT * INTO _offer FROM public.booking_offers WHERE id = _offer_id AND provider = 'stripe' AND active;
  IF NOT FOUND OR _offer.credits <= 0 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'not_a_package_offer'; END IF;

  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.booking_commerce_events WHERE provider = 'stripe' AND provider_event_id = _provider_event_id AND event_type = 'paid') THEN
    SELECT * INTO _pass FROM public.booking_passes WHERE student_id = _student_id ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1;
    RETURN _pass;
  END IF;

  SELECT * INTO _pass FROM public.booking_passes WHERE student_id = _student_id ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE;
  IF FOUND THEN
    UPDATE public.booking_passes
      SET total_credits = total_credits + _offer.credits,
          status = 'active', expires_at = NULL
      WHERE id = _pass.id RETURNING * INTO _pass;
  ELSE
    INSERT INTO public.booking_passes (student_id, student_email, student_name, total_credits, used_credits, session_length_minutes, status)
      VALUES (_student_id, _email, COALESCE(_email, 'Student'), _offer.credits, 0, 70, 'active')
      RETURNING * INTO _pass;
  END IF;

  INSERT INTO public.booking_commerce_events (student_id, offer_id, provider, provider_event_id, event_type, email)
    VALUES (_student_id, _offer_id, 'stripe', _provider_event_id, 'paid', _email);
  RETURN _pass;
END;
$$;

  // A plain stack, not a flex-1 column: the queue sizes to its own content and
  // `main` does the scrolling. Letting the sections flex-shrink collapsed the
  // list and painted its cards over the section below it.
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 px-5 py-6 sm:px-6">
      <SignupQueue />

      <section className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_260px]">
        <div className="border border-black/12 bg-white/25 px-4 py-3.5">

          {isLoading ? (

          <dl className="space-y-1.5 text-[13px]">
            {[
              { label: 'Email', value: counts.email },
              { label: 'Calendar', value: counts.calendar },
              { label: 'Student work', value: counts.work },
            ].map(({ label, value }) => (
              <div key={label} className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-3">
                <dt className="text-black/50">{label}</dt>
                <dd className="tabular-nums">{isLoading ? '—' : value}</dd>
              </div>
            ))}
          </dl>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section>
        <DeskTodoRail
          items={items}
          loading={isLoading}
          onOpen={(path) => navigate(teacherRoomPathForSource(path))}
          onInsertInChat={(text) => setChatInsert({ id: Date.now(), text })}
        />

      {/* Interim home for the assistant. It moves into the shell's AI panel in
          Phase 3; until then Today keeps the composer so the queue's
          "draft this" action still has somewhere to land. */}

        <DeskChatPanel
          localPreview={localPreview}
          insert={chatInsert}
          onInsertConsumed={() => setChatInsert(null)}
        />
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { Check, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { ACCESS_TIERS, type AccessTier, type ManagedUser } from '@/lib/adminUsers';
import { listManagedUsers, setStudentTier } from '@/lib/accessTierClient';

export { setStudentTier };

export const SIGNUP_QUEUE_KEY = ['signup-queue'] as const;

/** Undecided accounts: no tier yet, not archived, not an admin. Newest first. */
export function signupQueueRows(users: ManagedUser[]): ManagedUser[] {
  return users
    .filter((u) => u.role !== 'admin' && !u.archived_at && !u.access_tier)
    .sort((a, b) => (b.created_at ?? '').localeCompare(a.created_at ?? ''));
}

// Verified Stripe + Cal.com commerce ingress. Every accepted response follows a
// durable database commit; raw provider payloads and Booker PII never enter logs.
import { createClient } from "npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2";
import Stripe from "npm:stripe@18.5.0";

import { insertEvent, listEvents, meetUrlFromEvent } from "../_shared/googleCalendar.ts";
import { hasConflictingCalendarEvent, SINGLE_SESSION, validateSingleSessionCheckout } from "../_shared/singleSessionCore.ts";
import { encryptEnvelope, generateCapabilityToken, hmacHex, sha256Hex } from "../_shared/singleSessionCrypto.ts";
import {
  listPaymentLinkLineItems,
  retrievePaymentLink,
} from "../_shared/stripe.ts";

const MAX_BODY_BYTES = 256 * 1024;
const APP_ORIGIN = Deno.env.get("APP_ORIGIN") || "https://lsatprep.study";

const json = (body: unknown, status = 200) => new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {
  status,
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "Cache-Control": "no-store" },
});

function requiredEnv(name: string): string {
  const value = Deno.env.get(name);
  if (!value) throw new Error(`${name.toLowerCase()}_not_configured`);
  return value;
}

function expectedLivemode(): boolean {
  const value = requiredEnv("STRIPE_LIVEMODE").toLowerCase();
  if (value !== "true" && value !== "false") throw new Error("stripe_livemode_invalid");
  return value === "true";
}

async function readRaw(req: Request): Promise<string> {
  const declared = Number(req.headers.get("content-length") || "0");
  if (declared > MAX_BODY_BYTES) throw new Error("payload_too_large");
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(await req.arrayBuffer());
  if (bytes.byteLength > MAX_BODY_BYTES) throw new Error("payload_too_large");
  return new TextDecoder().decode(bytes);
}

function safeEqual(a: string, b: string) {
  if (a.length !== b.length) return false;
  let difference = 0;
  for (let index = 0; index < a.length; index++) difference |= a.charCodeAt(index) ^ b.charCodeAt(index);
  return difference === 0;
}

async function verifyCal(raw: string, signature: string | null) {
  const secret = requiredEnv("CALCOM_BOOKING_WEBHOOK_SECRET");
  return !!signature && safeEqual(await hmacHex(secret, raw), signature.replace(/^sha256=/i, ""));
}

async function matchStudent(supabase: any, email: string | null, name: string | null) {
  if (email) {
    const wanted = email.trim().toLowerCase();
    let page = 1;
    while (page <= 5) {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.admin.listUsers({ page, perPage: 200 });
      if (error) throw error;
      const users = data?.users ?? [];
      const hit = users.find((user: any) => (user.email ?? "").trim().toLowerCase() === wanted);
      if (hit) {
        const { data: profile, error: profileError } = await supabase
          .from("profiles").select("class_id,display_name").eq("class_id", hit.id).maybeSingle();
        if (profileError) throw profileError;
        if (profile) return { id: profile.class_id, display_name: profile.display_name, email: hit.email };
      }
      if (users.length < 200) break;
      page++;
    }
  }
  if (name) {
    const normalized = name.trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from("profiles").select("class_id,display_name").ilike("display_name", normalized).limit(2);
    if (error) throw error;
    if ((data ?? []).length === 1) return { id: data[0].class_id, display_name: data[0].display_name, email };
  }
  return null;
}

// Admin-only booking pass management: create / list / revoke / rotate.
// Every issued link uses the strong /book/:slug public route.
import { createClient } from "npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2";
import { corsHeaders } from "npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2/cors";
import { generateBookingSlug, validateNewPassInput } from "../_shared/bookingPassLinks.ts";
import { deleteEvent } from "../_shared/googleCalendar.ts";
import { findAuthUserIdByEmail } from "../_shared/linkPassToAccount.ts";

async function sha256Hex(input: string): Promise<string> {
  const buf = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(input));
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(buf)).map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}

function genToken(): string {
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(32);
  crypto.getRandomValues(bytes);
  return btoa(String.fromCharCode(...bytes))
    .replace(/\+/g, "-").replace(/\//g, "_").replace(/=+$/g, "");
}

const APP_ORIGIN = Deno.env.get("APP_ORIGIN") || "https://lsatprep.study";

function bookingUrl(slug: string): string {
  return `${APP_ORIGIN}/book/${slug}`;
}

    status,
    headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  });
}

  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });

    const authHeader = req.headers.get("Authorization") ?? "";
    if (!authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ")) return json({ error: "unauthorized" }, 401);

      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!,
      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")!,
    );

      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!,
      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_ANON_KEY")!,
    );
    const { data: claimsRes } = await authClient.auth.getClaims(token);

      .from("user_roles").select("role").eq("user_id", user.id).eq("role", "admin").maybeSingle();
    if (roleError) throw roleError;
    if (!roleRow) return json({ error: "forbidden" }, 403);

    const action = String(body.action || "");

teacher can see the ledger is current.\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.stripe_sync_runs (\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n  started_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  finished_at timestamptz,\n  since timestamptz NOT NULL,\n  charges_seen integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n  rows_written integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n  error text\n);\nALTER TABLE public.stripe_sync_runs provider_event_id, event_type) DO NOTHING;\n\n  UPDATE public.stripe_payments\n    SET grant_state = 'granted', granted_credits = _offer.credits, granted_at = now(), granted_by = auth.uid(), public.stripe_payments\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\nDECLARE _row public.stripe_payments%ROWTYPE;\nBEGIN\n  IF NOT public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin') THEN RAISE Shared by booking-commerce-webhook\n// (live events) and stripe-sync (backfill + refresh).\n//\n// Upserts never overwrite a teacher decision: a linked student, an ignored\n// payer, or a granted/dismissed package stays as the teacher left it. Only the\n// Stripe facts (status, refunds, payer details) refresh.\n\nconst STRIPE_API = \"https://api.stripe.com/v1\";\n\nexport async function stripeGet(secret: string, path: string, params: Record<string, string | string[]> = {}) {\n  const qs = new URLSearchParams();\n  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(params)) {\n    if (Array.isArray(value)) value.forEach((v) => qs.append(key, v));\n    else qs.append(key, value);\n  }\n  const res = await fetch(`${STRIPE_API}${path}${qs.size ? `?${qs}` : \"\"}`, {\n    headers: { Authorization: [REDACTED] ${secret}` },\n  });\n  const body = await res.json();\n  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`stripe_${res.status}_${body?.error?.code ?? body?.error?.type ?? \"error\"}`);\n  return body;\n}\n\n/** Every page of a Stripe list endpoint, oldest cap by `created[gte]`. */\nexport async function stripeListAll(secret: string, path: string, params: Record<string, string | string[]>, max = 5000) {\n  const out: any[] = [];\n  let startingAfter: string | null = null;\n  while (out.length < max) {\n    const page = await stripeGet(secret, path, {\n      ...params,\n      limit: \"100\",\n      ...(startingAfter ? { starting_after: startingAfter } : {}),\n    });\n    out.push(...(page.data ?? []));\n    if (!page.has_more || !page.data?.length) break;\n    startingAfter = page.data[page.data.length - 1].id;\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\nexport function chargeStatus(charge: any): string {\n  if (charge.disputed) return \"disputed\";\n  if (charge.status === \"failed\") return \"failed\";\n  if (charge.status === \"pending\") return \"pending\";\n  if (charge.refunded) return \"refunded\";\n  if ((charge.amount_refunded ?? 0) > 0) return \"partially_refunded\";\n  return \"succeeded\";\n}\n\nexport type StudentIndex = Map<string, string>;\n\n/** Lowercased email -> student id, for everyone with a profile. */\nexport async function loadStudentIndex(supabase: any): Promise<StudentIndex> {\n  const index: StudentIndex = new Map();\n  const { data: profiles, error } = await supabase.from(\"profiles\").select(\"class_id\");\n  if (error) throw error;\n  const hasProfile = new Set((profiles ?? []).map((p: any) => p.class_id));\n  for (let page = 1; page <= 20; page++) {\n    const { data, error: usersError } = await supabase.auth.admin.listUsers({ page, perPage: 200 });\n    if (usersError) throw usersError;\n    const users = data?.users ?? [];\n    for (const u of users) {\n      if (u.email && hasProfile.has(u.id)) index.set(u.email.trim().toLowerCase(), u.id);\n    }\n    if (users.length < 200) break;\n  }\n  return index;\n}\n\n/** Stripe payment link id -> active package offer (id, credits). */\nexport async function loadOfferIndex(supabase: any): Promise<Map<string, { id: string; credits: number }>> {\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.from(\"booking_offers\")\n    .select(\"id,credits,provider_ref\").eq(\"provider\", \"stripe\").eq(\"active\", true);\n  if (error) throw error;\n  return new Map((data ?? []).map((o: any) => [o.provider_ref, { id: o.id, credits: o.credits }]));\n}\n\nexport function buildPaymentRow(\n  charge: any,\n  session: any | null,\n  students: StudentIndex,\n  offers: Map<string, { id: string; credits: number }>,\n  source: \"webhook\" | \"backfill\",\n) {\n  const email: string | null =\n    session?.customer_details?.email ?? charge.billing_details?.email ?? charge.receipt_email ?? session?.customer_email ?? null;\n  const paymentLink = typeof session?.payment_link === \"string\" ? session.payment_link : session?.payment_link?.id ?? null;\n  const offer = paymentLink ? offers.get(paymentLink) : undefined;\n  const studentId = email ? students.get(email.trim().toLowerCase()) ?? null : null;\n  const status = chargeStatus(charge);\n  const isPackage = !!offer && offer.credits > 0;\n  return {\n    stripe_charge_id: charge.id,\n    payment_intent_id: typeof charge.payment_intent === \"string\" ? charge.payment_intent : charge.payment_intent?.id ?? null,\n    checkout_session_id: session?.id ?? null,\n    payment_link_id: paymentLink,\n    customer_id: typeof charge.customer === \"string\" ? charge.customer : charge.customer?.id ?? null,\n    email,\n    payer_name: session?.customer_details?.name ?? charge.billing_details?.name ?? null,\n    description: charge.description ?? null,\n    product: session?.metadata?.product ?? (isPackage ? \"package\" : null),\n    amount_cents: charge.amount,\n    amount_refunded_cents: charge.amount_refunded ?? 0,\n    currency: String(charge.currency ?? \"\").toLowerCase(),\n    status,\n    paid_at: new Date(charge.created * 1000).toISOString(),\n    livemode: !!charge.livemode,\n    student_id: studentId,\n    match_state: studentId ? \"matched\" : \"unmatched\",\n    offer_id: offer?.id ?? null,\n    // Backfilled history never asks for a grant: old packages were handled by hand.\n    grant_state: isPackage && studentId && status === \"succeeded\" && source === \"webhook\" ? \"awaiting\" : \"none\",\n    source,\n  };\n}\n\n/** Insert, or refresh Stripe facts on an existing row without touching teacher decisions. */\nexport async function upsertPayment(supabase: any, row: ReturnType<typeof buildPaymentRow>) {\n  const { data: existing, error } = await supabase.from(\"stripe_payments\")\n    .select(\"id,match_state,grant_state,student_id,source\").eq(\"stripe_charge_id\", row.stripe_charge_id).maybeSingle();\n  if (error) throw error;\n  if (!existing) {\n    const { error: insertError } = await supabase.from(\"stripe_payments\").insert(row);\n    // A concurrent writer (webhook vs sync) inserted first: fall through to update.\n    if (!insertError) return;\n    if (insertError.code !== \"23505\") throw insertError;\n    return upsertPayment(supabase, row);\n  }\n  const facts: Record<string, unknown> = {\n    payment_intent_id: row.payment_intent_id,\n    checkout_session_id: row.checkout_session_id ?? undefined,\n    payment_link_id: row.payment_link_id ?? undefined,\n    customer_id: row.customer_id,\n    email: row.email,\n    payer_name: row.payer_name,\n    description: row.description,\n    product: row.product ?? undefined,\n    amount_cents: row.amount_cents,\n    amount_refunded_cents: row.amount_refunded_cents,\n    currency: row.currency,\n    status: row.status,\n    offer_id: row.offer_id ?? undefined,\n    updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n  };\n  if (existing.match_state === \"unmatched\" && row.student_id) {\n    facts.student_id = row.student_id;\n    facts.match_state = \"matched\";\n  }\n  // A live event arriving for a row first seen by backfill may still ask for a grant.\n  if (existing.grant_state === \"none\" && row.grant_state === \"awaiting\") facts.grant_state = \"awaiting\";\n  for (const k of Object.keys(facts)) if (facts[k] === undefined) delete facts[k];\n  const { error: updateError } = await supabase.from(\"stripe_payments\").update(facts).eq(\"id\", existing.id);\n  if (updateError) throw updateError;\n}\n\n/** Record one charge (by id) into the ledger, looking up its Checkout Session. */\nexport async function recordCharge(supabase: any, secret: string, chargeId: string, source: \"webhook\" | \"backfill\") {\n  const charge = await stripeGet(secret, `/charges/${chargeId}`);\n  let session: any = null;\n  const pi = typeof charge.payment_intent === \"string\" ? paid_at: string;\n  student_id: string | null;\n  match_state: 'matched' | 'unmatched' | 'linked' | 'ignored';\n  offer_id: string | null;\n  grant_state: 'none' | 'awaiting' | 'granted' | ').toLowerCase();\n  return q.toLowerCase().split(/\\s+/).every((w) => hay.includes(w));\n}\n\nconst STATUS_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {\n  succeeded: 'Paid',\n  pending: 'Pending',\n  failed: = useQueryClient();\n  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');\n  const [shown, setShown] = useState(PAGE);\n  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [linking, setLinking] = []) m.set(s.class_id, s.display_name || s.email);\n    return m;\n  }, [students.data]);\n  const studentName = (id: string | null) => (id ? nameById.get(id) ?? null : null);\n\n  const rows = payments.data ?? [];\n  const attention = rows.filter(needsAttention);\n  const filtered = rows.filter((p) => matchesSearch(p, query.trim(), studentName));\n  const paidTotal = rows\n    .filter((p) => p.status === 'succeeded' || p.status === 'partially_refunded')\n    .reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.amount_cents - p.amount_refunded_cents, 0);\n\n  const refresh = () => {\n    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: PAYMENTS_KEY });\n    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: SYNC_KEY });\n  };\n\n  const sync = async () => {\n    setSyncing(true);\n    try {\n      const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke('stripe-sync', { body: { days: 90 } });\n      if (error) throw error;\n      toast.success(`Synced ${data?.charges ?? 0} Stripe charges`);\n      refresh();\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error('stripe-sync failed', error);\n      toast.error('Stripe sync failed');\n    } finally {\n      setSyncing(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const rpc = async (p: StripePayment, fn: string, args: Record<string, unknown>, done: string) => {\n    setBusy(p.id);\n    try {\n      const { error } = await supabase.rpc(fn as any, { _payment_id: p.id, ...args } as any);\n      if (error) throw error;\n      toast.success(done);\n      setLinking(null);\n      refresh();\n    } catch (error: any) {\n      console.error(`${fn} failed`, { id: p.id, error });\n      toast.error(error?.message ?? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />\n            {syncing ? 'Syncing' : 'Sync from Stripe'}\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {attention.length > 0 && (\n        <div className=\"mb-3 border border-amber-700/25 bg-amber-500/5 px-3 py-2\">\n          <div className=\"mb-1.5 text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-amber-900/70\">\n            Needs you &middot; {attention.length}\n          </div>\n          <ul className=\"divide-y divide-black/10\">\n            {attention.map((p) => (\n              <li key={p.id} className=\"py-2\" data-testid={`payment-attention-${p.id}`}>\n                <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-[13px]\">\n                  <span className=\"min-w-0 flex-1 truncate\">\n                    <span className=\"font-semibold\">{who(p)}</span>{' '}\n                    <span className=\"text-black/50\">paid {money(p.amount_cents, p.currency)} on {new Date(p.paid_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>\n                    {p.grant_state === 'awaiting' && <span className=\"text-black/50\"> &middot; package</span>}\n                  </span>\n                  {p.grant_state === 'awaiting' ? (\n                    <button\n                      type=\"button\"\n                      disabled={busy === p.id}\n                      onClick={() => rpc(p, 'admin_grant_stripe_payment', {}, `Passes added for ${who(p)}`)}\n                      className=\"border border-emerald-700/30 px-2 py-0.5 text-[12px] text-emerald-800 hover:bg-emerald-500/10 disabled:opacity-40\"\n                    >\n                      Grant passes\n                    </button>\n                  ) : (\n                    <button\n                      type=\"button\"\n                      disabled={busy === p.id}\n                      onClick={() => setLinking(linking === p.id ? null : p.id)}\n                      aria-expanded={linking === p.id}\n                      className=\"border border-black/20 px-2 py-0.5 text-[12px] text-black/70 hover:bg-black/5 disabled:opacity-40\"\n                    >\n                      Link student\n                    </button>\n                  )}\n                  <button\n                    type=\"button\"\n                    disabled={busy === p.id}\n                    onClick={() => rpc(p, 'admin_dismiss_stripe_payment', {}, 'Dismissed')}\n                    className=\"px-1 text-[12px] text-black/40 hover:text-black/70 disabled:opacity-40\"\n                  >\n                    Dismiss\n                  </button>\n                </div>\n                {p.email && <div className=\"font-mono text-[11px] text-black/40\">{p.email}</div>}\n                {linking === p.id && (\n                  <select\n                    aria-label={`Student who made the ${money(p.amount_cents, p.currency)} payment`}\n                    className=\"mt-1.5 w-full border border-black/15 bg-white px-2 py-1 text-[13px]\"\n                    defaultValue=\"\"\n                    onChange={(e) => e.target.value && rpc(p, 'admin_link_stripe_payment', { _student_id: e.target.value }, 'Linked')}\n                  >\n                    <option value=\"\" disabled>Choose a student</option>\n                    {(students.data ?? [])\n                      .slice()\n                      .sort((a, b) => (a.display_name || a.email).localeCompare(b.display_name || b.email))\n                      .map((s) => (\n                        <option key={s.class_id} value={s.class_id}>\n                          {s.display_name || s.email} ({s.email})\n                        </option>\n                      ))}\n                  </select>\n                )}\n              </li>\n            ))}\n          </ul>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      <input\n        type=\"search\"\n        value={query}\n        onChange={(e) => { setQuery(e.target.value); setShown(PAGE); }}\n        placeholder=\"Search name, email, amount\"\n        aria-label=\"Search payments\"\n        className=\"mb-2 w-full border border-black/15 bg-white/60 px-2 py-1 text-[13px] placeholder:text-black/35\"\n      />\n\n      {payments.isLoading ? (\n        <p className=\"text-[13px] text-black/45\">Loading</p>\n      ) : rows.length === 0 ? (\n        <p className=\"text-[13px] text-black/45\">No payments recorded yet. Sync from Stripe to load the last 90 days.</p>\n      ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (\n        <p className=\"text-[13px] text-black/45\">No payment matches that search.</p>\n      ) : (\n        <>\n          <ul className=\"divide-y divide-black/10\">\n            {filtered.slice(0, shown).map((p) => (\n              <li key={p.id} className=\"flex items-baseline gap-3 py-1.5 text-[13px]\">\n                <span className=\"w-[76px] shrink-0 tabular-nums text-black/50\">\n                  {new Date(p.paid_at).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })}\n                </span>\n                <span className=\"min-w-0 flex-1 truncate\">\n                  {who(p)}\n                  {p.product && <span className=\"text-black/40\"> &middot; {p.product.replace('_', ' ')}</span>}\n                  {p.grant_state === 'granted' && <span className=\"text-black/40\"> &middot; {p.granted_credits} passes granted</span>}\n                </span>\n                <span className={`shrink-0 text-[11px] ${p.status === 'succeeded' ? 'text-black/40' : 'text-red-800/80'}`}>\n                  {STATUS_LABEL[p.status] ?? p.status}\n                </span>\n                <span className=\"w-[80px] shrink-0 text-right tabular-nums\">{money(p.amount_cents, p.currency)}</span>\n              </li>\n            ))}\n          </ul>\n          {filtered.length > shown && (\n            <button\n              type=\"button\"\n              onClick={() => setShown((n) => n + PAGE)}\n              className=\"mt-2 text-[12px] text-black/50 underline-offset-2 hover:underline\"\n            >\n              Show more ({filtered.length - shown} left)\n            </button>\n          )}\n        </>\n      )}\n    </section>\n  );\n}\n"}

Keep Lovable credit spend minimal. Stated 2026-09-24, after the workspace ran out mid-deploy.

**Why:** credits ran out halfway through the access-tiers rollout; he topped up and asked to conserve from now on.

**How to apply:**
- Free paths first: migrations and data fixes via `query_database` (direct SQL, no agent), frontend publish via `deploy_project`, code via git push/PR.
- The only thing that truly needs the Lovable agent is deploying Edge Functions. Batch every pending function into ONE short, backend-only message ("deploy X and Y exactly as on main, edit no code"), never a exploratory or multi-turn chat.
- Never use the Lovable agent to read code, verify, or investigate; do that locally or with `query_database`.
- Still confirm with Joshua immediately before any credit-spending message (see [[lovable-frontend-publish-is-separate]], [[lovable-edge-functions-do-not-deploy-from-push]]).

UNIQUE,\n  payment_intent_id text,\n  checkout_session_id text,\n  payment_link_id text,\n  customer_id text,\n  email text,\n  payer_name text,\n  description text,\n  product text,\n  amount_cents integer NOT NULL,\n  amount_refunded_cents integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n  currency text NOT NULL,\n  status text NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('succeeded','pending','failed','refunded','partially_refunded','disputed')),\n  paid_at timestamptz NOT NULL,\n  livemode boolean NOT NULL,\n  student_id uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\n  match_state text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'unmatched' CHECK (match_state IN ('matched','unmatched','linked','ignored')),\n  offer_id text REFERENCES public.booking_offers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\n  grant_state text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'none' CHECK (grant_state IN ('none','awaiting','granted','dismissed')),\n  granted_credits integer,\n  granted_at timestamptz,\n  granted_by uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\n  source text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'webhook' CHECK (source IN ('webhook','backfill')),\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS stripe_payments_paid_at_idx ON public.stripe_payments (paid_at DESC);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS stripe_payments_student_idx ON public.stripe_payments (student_id);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS stripe_payments_email_idx ON public.stripe_payments (lower(email));\nALTER TABLE public.stripe_payments ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\nCREATE POLICY \"admins_read_stripe_payments\" ON public.stripe_payments\n  FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'));\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.stripe_sync_runs (\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n  started_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  finished_at timestamptz,\n  since timestamptz NOT NULL,\n  charges_seen integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n  rows_written integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n  error text\n);\nALTER TABLE public.stripe_sync_runs ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\nCREATE POLICY \"admins_read_stripe_sync_runs\" ON public.stripe_sync_runs\n  FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'));\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.admin_link_stripe_payment(_payment_id uuid, _student_id uuid)\nRETURNS public.stripe_payments\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\nDECLARE _row public.stripe_payments%ROWTYPE;\nBEGIN\n  IF NOT public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'forbidden'; END IF;\n  UPDATE public.stripe_payments\n    SET student_id = _student_id,\n        match_state = CASE WHEN _student_id IS NULL THEN 'ignored' ELSE 'linked' END,\n        grant_state = CASE\n          WHEN grant_state = 'none' AND offer_id IS NOT NULL AND _student_id IS NOT NULL AND status = 'succeeded' THEN 'awaiting'\n          ELSE grant_state END,\n        updated_at = now()\n    WHERE id = _payment_id RETURNING * INTO _row;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'unknown_payment'; END IF;\n  RETURN _row;\nEND;\n$$;\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.admin_grant_stripe_payment(_payment_id uuid)\nRETURNS public.stripe_payments\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\nDECLARE\n  _row public.stripe_payments%ROWTYPE;\n  _offer public.booking_offers%ROWTYPE;\n  _pass public.booking_passes%ROWTYPE;\nBEGIN\n  IF NOT public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'forbidden'; END IF;\n  SELECT * INTO _row FROM public.stripe_payments WHERE id = _payment_id FOR UPDATE;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'unknown_payment'; END IF;\n  IF _row.grant_state = 'granted' THEN RETURN _row; END IF;\n  IF _row.student_id IS NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'link_a_student_first'; END IF;\n  IF _row.status <> 'succeeded' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'payment_not_settled'; END IF;\n  SELECT * INTO _offer FROM public.booking_offers WHERE id = _row.offer_id AND provider = 'stripe';\n  IF NOT FOUND OR _offer.credits <= 0 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'not_a_package_offer'; END IF;\n  SELECT * INTO _pass FROM public.booking_passes WHERE student_id = _row.student_id ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE;\n  IF FOUND THEN\n    UPDATE public.booking_passes\n      SET total_credits = total_credits + _offer.credits, status = 'active', expires_at = NULL\n      WHERE id = _pass.id;\n  ELSE\n    INSERT INTO public.booking_passes (student_id, student_email, student_name, total_credits, used_credits, session_length_minutes, status)\n      VALUES (_row.student_id, _row.email, COALESCE(_row.payer_name, _row.email, 'Student'), _offer.credits, 0, 70, 'active');\n  END IF;\n  INSERT INTO public.booking_commerce_events (student_id, offer_id, provider, provider_event_id, event_type, email, amount_cents, currency)\n    VALUES (_row.student_id, _row.offer_id, 'stripe', 'charge:' || _row.stripe_charge_id, 'paid', _row.email, _row.amount_cents, _row.currency)\n    ON CONFLICT (provider, provider_event_id, event_type) DO NOTHING;\n  UPDATE public.stripe_payments\n    SET grant_state = 'granted', granted_credits = _offer.credits, granted_at = now(), granted_by = auth.uid(), updated_at = now()\n    WHERE id = _payment_id RETURNING * INTO _row;\n  RETURN _row;\nEND;\n$$;\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.admin_dismiss_stripe_payment(_payment_id uuid)\nRETURNS public.stripe_payments\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\nDECLARE _row public.stripe_payments%ROWTYPE;\nBEGIN\n  IF NOT public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'forbidden'; END IF;\n  UPDATE public.stripe_payments\n    SET grant_state = CASE WHEN grant_state = 'awaiting' THEN 'dismissed' ELSE grant_state END,\n        match_state = CASE WHEN match_state = 'unmatched' THEN 'ignored' ELSE match_state END,\n        updated_at = now()\n    WHERE id = _payment_id RETURNING * INTO _row;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'unknown_payment'; END IF;\n  RETURN _row;\nEND;\n$$;\nREVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.admin_link_stripe_payment(uuid, uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon;\nREVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.admin_grant_stripe_payment(uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon;\nREVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.admin_dismiss_stripe_payment(uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon;\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.admin_link_stripe_payment(uuid, uuid) TO authenticated;\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.admin_grant_stripe_payment(uuid) TO authenticated;\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.admin_dismiss_stripe_payment(uuid) TO authenticated;"}

select charge,pi,cs,link,cust,email,name,descr,product,amt,refunded,cur,status,paid_at::timestamptz,live,offer,
  m.id, case when m.id is null then 'unmatched' else 'matched' end, 'none', 'backfill'
from src left join lateral (select u.id from auth.users u join public.profiles p on p.class_id=u.id where lower(u.email)=lower(src.email) limit 1) m on true
on conflict (stripe_charge_id) do nothing;
insert into public.stripe_sync_runs (since, finished_at, charges_seen, rows_written) values (now() - interval '90 days', now(), 37, 37);

I built the Stripe connection and loaded your history, but the part you'll see and use isn't live yet. It needs a Lovable deploy (your prompt is below), and I couldn't check the new Payments panel in the browser. The local test page sent me to the sign-in screen, and I don't enter your password. Nothing is merged to `main`.

**Done and checked:**
- **Database (live):** I created the payments table, a sync log, and three admin-only actions: link a student, grant passes, dismiss. This went through Lovable's direct SQL route, so no credits were used.
- **Three months of history (live):** I pulled 37 Stripe charges since about 2026-06-29. 33 matched a student automatically, and the net total is $5,929. Old charges never trigger a pass grant.
- **Code:** [PR #143](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/pull/143). The typecheck passes and 5 new unit tests pass.
  - A Payments panel on your Today page: search, a "Sync from Stripe" button, and a "Needs you" list.
  - The webhook now records every charge, refund and dispute.
  - Package payments no longer add passes on their own. They show up waiting for your one-click grant.
  - Webhook errors now log the real cause instead of just "provider_processing_failed".

**Correction to the last session's handoff:** it said the webhook gives platform access for any paid Checkout. That came from an out-of-date local copy. On `main`, a payment already never changes access. The real gap was package payments adding passes automatically, and that's what I fixed.

**Your part in Lovable.** Merge PR #143 first, then paste this into Lovable. It spends credits, and it's the only step that needs the agent:

```
Deploy these two Supabase Edge Functions exactly as they are on main. Do not edit any code, schema, or other files.
1. stripe-sync (new; verify_jwt = true, already in supabase/config.toml)
2. booking-commerce-webhook (updated)
Both import supabase/functions/_shared/stripeLedger.ts. The database migration is already applied. Reply only with the deploy result for each function.
```

**After the functions deploy:**
1. I publish the frontend with `deploy_project`. It's free and already approved.
2. You sign in on the preview pane (port 8093) or on the live site, and I check the panel.
3. I add `charge.succeeded`, `charge.refunded`, `charge.failed` and `charge.dispute.created` to your existing Stripe webhook. That changes a Stripe setting, so say yes and I'll do it through the API. Until then, the webhook still records Checkout payments and refunds.

**Still open:**
- The Codex Stripe connector needs you to sign in again. Nothing above depends on it.
- The cause of the 2026-09-21 HTTP 500 is still unknown. The better logging will name the cause if it happens again.
- 4 of the 37 charges have no matching student account. They're in the list but won't ask you for anything, because old charges were already handled by hand.

**How it's set up now**
- **API key: already at maximum.** LSAT U's key is a full, unrestricted live secret key for Aspiring Attorneys (`acct_1PJnXRIULVCj6rqq`). Charges and payouts are both enabled. Every read I tried worked: payments, customers, invoices, payouts, balance, disputes, events, products and payment links. There's no more access to grant. It could only be narrowed.
- **Webhook: I widened it.** It had 5 event types (Checkout completed or expired, and refunds). It now has 15, adding every charge event, disputes, and delayed Checkout payments. I made the change through the API and Stripe confirmed it. The webhook running today answers any event it doesn't handle with a plain 200, so nothing breaks before the new code deploys.

**The main gap: most of your package links aren't recognized**
You have 100 active payment links. LSAT U knows only 2 of them as packages:
- "5 hours" at $200
- "10 hour reduced" at $350

Every other hour package is recorded as a payment, but it will never show a "Grant passes" button. Recent examples:
- 11 Hour Package, $500, paid 2026-09-25
- LSAT 10 hours, $500
- 5 hours tutoring, $225
- 20 hours, 15 hours, 5 hour Canadian link, and more

**Fix:** register each link that's still in use as a package, with its hour count as the number of credits. This is a free SQL change. My guess, from how your two existing packages are set up, is 1 hour = 1 pass credit. Tell me which links are still live, or say "all hour links" and I'll map every one of them. A lot of these are old one-offs, so I'd rather not guess which to keep.

**Smaller gap:** the $45 single sessions booked through Cal.com arrive without Checkout details. They're recorded with the payer's email but no product label. That's fine for the feed and doesn't block anything.

**Still waiting on you, from before**

{"command": "PYTHONIOENCODING=utf-8 python - <<'EOF'\nimport json,urllib.request,urllib.parse,pathlib,time,collections\nkey=[l.split(\"=\",1)[1].strip().strip('\"\\'') for l in pathlib.Path(\".env\").read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines() if l.startswith(\"STRIPE_SECRET_KEY=\")][0]\ndef g(p,params={}):\n    out=[];after=None\n    while True:\n        q=dict(params,limit=100,**({\"starting_after\":after} if after else {}))\n        d=json.load(urllib.request.urlopen(urllib.request.Request(\"https://api.stripe.com/v1\"+p+\"?\"+urllib.parse.urlencode(q),headers={\"Authorization\":\"Bearer \"+key})))\n        if \"data\" not in d: return d\n        out+=d[\"data\"]\n        if not d[\"has_more\"]: return out\n        after=d[\"data\"][-1][\"id\"]\nsince=int(time.time())-240*86400\nlinks=g(\"/payment_links\",{\"active\":\"true\"})\nsess=g(\"/checkout/sessions\",{\"created[gte]\":since,\"status\":\"complete\"})\nuse=collections.defaultdict(list)\nfor s in sess:\n    if s.get(\"payment_link\") and s.get(\"payment_status\")==\"paid\": use[s[\"payment_link\"]].append(s)\nrows=[]\nfor l in links:\n    li=g(f\"/payment_links/{l['id']}/line_items\")\n    name=\" + \".join(x.get(\"description\") or \"?\" for x in li); amt=sum(x.get(\"amount_total\",0) for x in li)\n    u=use.get(l[\"id\"],[])\n    last=max((s[\"created\"] for s in u),default=None)\n    rows.append((len(u),last,name,amt,l[\"id\"],l.get(\"url\")))\nrows.sort(key=lambda r:(-(r[1] or 0)))\nprint(\"USED since\",time.strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d\",time.gmtime(since)))\nfor n,last,name,amt,i,url in rows:\n    if n: print(f\"{n}x | last {time.strftime('%Y-%m-%d',time.gmtime(last))} | {name} | ${amt/100:.0f} | {i}\")\nprint(\"\\nUNUSED\")\nfor n,last,name,amt,i,url in sorted(rows,key=lambda r:r[2].lower()):\n    if not n: print(f\"{name} | ${amt/100:.0f} | {i}\")\nprint(\"\\ntotal active\",len(links))\nEOF", "description": "Classify active payment links by use in last 8 months", "timeout": 300000}

3:-- 1. The native-offer cutover and get_effective_booking_offers from
4---    20260816030000 never reached production, so student-booking resolved no
5---    offers at all and students saw an empty purchase list. The offer registry
6---    and resolver are re-stated here idempotently. The worker/outbox half of
7---    that migration was already applied and is deliberately not repeated.
8---
9--- 2. resolve_booking_link_full RETURNS booking_passes, so a non-resolving slug
10---    returns an all-NULL composite rather than an empty result. Over PostgREST
11---    that is a truthy object, so public-booking treated it as a real pass and
12---    filtered redemptions on uuid = 'null', turning every non-resolving link
13---    into an HTTP 500. The callers now check pass.id (see the edge functions),
14---    and an 'exhausted' pass resolves so a spent link renders its own page
15---    instead of reading as invalid.

STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
  WITH arrangement AS (
    SELECT EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM public.student_booking_offers WHERE student_id = _student_id
    ) AS has_rows
  ), default_offers AS (
    SELECT o.id, o.label, o.provider, o.provider_ref,
      o.url, o.credits, true AS enabled,
      false AS is_legacy_external_override
    FROM public.booking_offers o
    CROSS JOIN arrangement a
    WHERE NOT a.has_rows AND o.active
  ), arranged_offers AS (
    SELECT o.id, o.label, o.provider, o.provider_ref,
      COALESCE(NULLIF(trim(s.url_override), ''), o.url) AS url,
      o.credits, true AS enabled,
      (o.id = 'single-session' AND NULLIF(trim(s.url_override), '') IS NOT NULL
        AND trim(s.url_override) NOT LIKE '/%') AS is_legacy_external_override
    FROM public.student_booking_offers s
    JOIN public.booking_offers o ON o.id = s.offer_id
    WHERE s.student_id = _student_id AND s.enabled
      AND (o.active OR (o.id = 'single-session'
        AND NULLIF(trim(s.url_override), '') IS NOT NULL
        AND trim(s.url_override) NOT LIKE '/%'))
  )
  SELECT * FROM default_offers
  UNION ALL
  SELECT * FROM arranged_offers;
$$;

SELECT student_id, 'single-session-native', enabled,
  CASE WHEN NULLIF(trim(url_override), '') IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE url_override END,
  created_at, now()
FROM public.student_booking_offers
WHERE offer_id = 'single-session'

true, false),\n  ('stripe-5h-275',         '5 hour ($275)',                          'stripe', 'plink_1ShEAKIULVCj6rqqqu44Crde', NULL, 5,  true, false),\n  ('stripe-5h-200-c',       '5 hours tutoring ($200, older link)',    'stripe', 'plink_1RgZIpIULVCj6rqqY7j529rm', NULL, 5,  true, false),\n  ('stripe-4h-260',         'LSAT Tutoring Package, 4 hours ($260)',  'stripe', 'plink_1T8Kg5IULVCj6rqqiwKsbOVD', NULL, 4,  true, false)\nON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_effective_booking_offers(_student_id uuid)\nRETURNS TABLE (\n  id text, label text, provider text, provider_ref text, url text,\n  credits integer, enabled boolean, is_legacy_external_override boolean\n)\nLANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public\nAS $$\n  WITH arrangement AS (\n    SELECT EXISTS (\n      SELECT 1 FROM public.student_booking_offers WHERE student_id = _student_id\n    ) AS has_rows\n  ), default_offers AS (\n    SELECT o.id, o.label, o.provider, o.provider_ref,\n      o.url, o.credits, true AS enabled,\n      false AS is_legacy_external_override\n    FROM public.booking_offers o\n    CROSS JOIN arrangement a\n    WHERE NOT a.has_rows AND o.active AND o.student_visible\n  ), arranged_offers AS (\n    SELECT o.id, o.label, o.provider, o.provider_ref,\n      COALESCE(NULLIF(trim(s.url_override), ''), o.url) AS url,\n      o.credits, true AS enabled,\n      (o.id = 'single-session' AND NULLIF(trim(s.url_override), '') IS NOT NULL\n        AND trim(s.url_override) NOT LIKE '/%') AS is_legacy_external_override\n    FROM public.student_booking_offers s\n    JOIN public.booking_offers o ON o.id = s.offer_id\n    WHERE s.student_id = _student_id AND s.enabled\n      AND (o.active OR (o.id = 'single-session'\n        AND NULLIF(trim(s.url_override), '') IS NOT NULL\n        AND trim(s.url_override) NOT LIKE '/%'))\n  )\n  SELECT * FROM default_offers\n  UNION ALL\n  SELECT * FROM arranged_offers;\n$$;\n\n-- Label historical ledger rows with their package (history never asks for a grant).\nUPDATE public.stripe_payments p\n  SET offer_id = o.id, product = COALESCE(p.product, 'package'), updated_at = now()\n ## assistant

I registered all 10 hour-package links as packages in the live LSAT U database, at 1 pass credit per hour. From now on, any payment through one of them shows a "Grant passes" button on your Today page. It's the same as before: nothing is granted until you click.

| Link | Credits |
|---|---|
| 11 Hour Package ($500) | 11 |
| LSAT 10 hours ($500) | 10 |
| 10 hour reduced ($350) | 10 (already registered) |
| 5 hours ($200) | 5 (already registered) |
| 5 hours tutoring ($225) | 5 |
| 5 hour tutoring, reduced ($200) | 5 |
| 5 hours ($200, second link) | 5 |
| 5 hour ($275) | 5 |
| 5 hours tutoring ($200, older link) | 5 |
| LSAT Tutoring Package, 4 hours ($260) | 4 |

**Students' purchase options are unchanged.** LSAT U offers every active package to all students who don't have their own arrangement. I checked the result: a student without an arrangement still sees only the 5-hour package, the 10-hour package and the single session.

**Your payment history is labeled.** The 19 past charges made through these links now show as packages in your ledger. None of them asks for a grant, since those were handled by hand.

  // Every charge lands in the teacher's payments ledger. The ledger is a view,
  // never a trigger, so a failure here is logged and never makes Stripe retry.
  const ledgerChargeId = await chargeIdForLedger(event, stripeSecret).catch(() => null);
  if (ledgerChargeId) {
    try {
      await recordCharge(supabase, stripeSecret, ledgerChargeId, "webhook");
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("ledger write failed", { event: event.id, type: event.type, error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err) });
    }
    if (event.type.startsWith("charge.")) return json({ received: true });
  }

  if (event.type.startsWith("refund.")) {
    const refund = event.data.object as Stripe.Refund;
    if (event.livemode !== expectedLivemode()) return json({ error: "livemode_mismatch" }, 400);
  const session = event.data.object;

  // A payment never changes access. Access is Joshua's decision, made by tier
  // on the People page, and it never changes by itself. What a payment does is
  // tell him, so a paying student is not left waiting unnoticed in the queue.
  if (session.payment_status === "paid") {
    try {
      await notifyAdminsOfPayment(supabase, {
        email: session.customer_details?.email ?? session.customer_email ?? null,
        name: session.customer_details?.name ?? null,
        amountCents: typeof session.amount_total === "number" ? session.amount_total : null,
        currency: session.currency ?? null,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      // A notice is a convenience on top of the payment record. Failing to
      // write it must never make Stripe retry a payment that was recorded.
      console.error("payment notice failed", { event: event.id, err });
    }
  }

  if (session.metadata?.product === "single_session") {
    const bookingId = String(session.metadata?.booking_id || "");
    if (!bookingId) return json({ error: "invalid_product" }, 400);


/** One notification per admin: someone paid, decide their tier. */
async function notifyAdminsOfPayment(
  supabase: { from: (t: string) => any },
  payment: { email: string | null; name: string | null; amountCents: number | null; currency: string | null },
) {
  const { data: admins, error } = await supabase.from("user_roles").select("user_id").eq("role", "admin");
  if (error) throw error;
  const who = payment.name || payment.email || "Someone";
  const amount =
    payment.amountCents !== null
      ? ` ${(payment.amountCents / 100).toFixed(2)} ${(payment.currency ?? "").toUpperCase()}`.trimEnd()
      : "";
  const rows = (admins ?? []).map((a: { user_id: string }) => ({
    user_id: a.user_id,
    type: "payment_needs_tier",
    title: "Payment received",
    body: `${who}${payment.email && payment.name ? ` (${payment.email})` : ""} paid${amount}. Their access has not changed. Set their tier if they need more.`,
    link: "/teacher/students",
  }));
  if (rows.length === 0) return;
  const { error: insertError } = await supabase.from("notifications").insert(rows);
  // A failed notice must not abort the webhook: the payment itself is already
  // recorded, and throwing here would make the provider retry the whole event.
  if (insertError) console.error("payment notice failed", { error: insertError });

## assistant

The code fix is ready in [PR #145](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/pull/145). Short answer: **not perfectly yet.** Right now some payments will tell your admin account within seconds, and some won't tell you at all. It needs one Lovable deploy (below) to fix.

## What happens today when someone pays

1. **Stripe sends the payment to LSAT U within a few seconds.** The webhook is live and verified, and it now listens for 15 event types. Every charge lands in your Payments ledger on Today.
2. **Whether you get a notification depends on how they paid:**

| How they paid | In the Payments ledger? | Notification to your admin account? |
|---|---|---|
| Payment link (packages) | Yes, within seconds | Yes |
| $45 native single session (/book/session) | Yes | Yes |
| **$45 session booked through Cal.com** | Yes | **No. Nothing tells you.** |
| Refund or dispute | Yes, the status updates | No |

3. **Nothing changes on its own.** Passes and access stay the same until you act. A package payment waits under "Needs you" for your Grant click.

## What I found and fixed

- **Notifications weren't reaching your screen live. Fixed.** The notifications table was never set to push changes to the browser, so a payment notice only showed after a reload or a refetch. I turned that on for both notifications and payments. It's free SQL, it's live now, and I checked it.
- **There has never been a single payment notification.** I checked the database: zero payment notices, even though Adrienne paid on 2026-09-25 and Gianna on 2026-09-22. The version of the webhook running then evidently didn't have the notification code. Lovable's deploy tonight is the first to include it. So payment-link notifications are only live as of that deploy, and none has actually fired yet, since no one has paid since.
- **Cal.com bookings never notify you. Fixed in PR #145, not deployed yet.** The notification now fires once for every new paid charge, whatever it paid for. The message says when a package is waiting for your grant or when the payer doesn't match a student, and it links to Today. The Payments panel also refreshes by itself when a payment arrives. The unit tests pass and the webhook and sync code compile. The app typecheck ran too, but I cut off its output, so I can't confirm it passed.

## To make it complete

Merge PR #145, or say "merge it" and I will, then paste this into Lovable. It spends credits:

```
Deploy the Supabase Edge Function booking-commerce-webhook exactly as it is on main. Do not edit any code, schema, or other files. Reply only with the deploy result.
```

After that I publish the frontend (free) for the live-updating panel.

**This is in-app only.** You'll see it in LSAT U when you're signed in, within a few seconds of the payment. It won't text or email you. If you want an email to contact@aspiringattorneys.com for each payment, I can add that next. It's a small change in the same function, and it could go out in the same deploy if you decide before you paste the prompt.

**Still unproven end to end:** a real payment going all the way through. The $1 "Tester" link is still active. Pay it once after the deploy and I'll confirm the ledger row and the notification landed and how fast.

import { createClient } from 'npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2'
import {
  sendTemplateEmail,
  type SendTemplateEmailResult,
} from './transactional-email-templates/send-email.ts'

/**
 * Shared app-email send path.
 *
 * Sends a registered template through Lovable's managed email API and keeps the
 * project's own `email_send_log` audit trail in sync:
 *   - `sent`       after a successful send
 *   - `suppressed` when the recipient is suppressed (bounce/complaint/unsubscribe)
 *   - `failed`     when the send throws
 *
 * Delivery, retries, rate limiting, suppression enforcement and the unsubscribe
 * footer are handled server-side, so there is nothing to enqueue here.
 */
export interface SendAppEmailOptions {
  templateData?: Record<string, unknown>
  idempotencyKey?: string
  replyTo?: string
}

function logClient() {
  const supabaseUrl = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')
  const serviceKey = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')
  if (!supabaseUrl || !serviceKey) return null
  return createClient(supabaseUrl, serviceKey)
}

async function writeLog(
  templateName: string,
  recipientEmail: string,
  status: 'sent' | 'suppressed' | 'failed',
  errorMessage?: string,
) {
  const supabase = logClient()
  if (!supabase) return
  const { error } = await supabase.from('email_send_log').insert({
    message_id: null,
    template_name: templateName,
    recipient_email: recipientEmail,
    status,
    error_message: errorMessage ?? null,
  })
  if (error) {
    console.error('email_send_log insert failed', { status, templateName, error })
  }
}

export async function sendAppEmail(
  templateName: string,
  recipientEmail: string,
  options: SendAppEmailOptions = {},
): Promise<SendTemplateEmailResult> {
  let result: SendTemplateEmailResult
  try {
    result = await sendTemplateEmail(templateName, recipientEmail, {
      templateData: options.templateData as Record<string, any> | undefined,

import * as React from 'npm:react@18.3.1'
import {
  Body, Container, Head, Heading, Html, Preview, Text,
} from 'npm:@react-email/components@0.0.22'
import type { TemplateEntry } from './registry.ts'

// The intake notification goes to Joshua, never to the student. The whole
// Markdown file sits in the body as preformatted text: it reads fine on a
// phone, and it is the exact text that gets filed into the student's folder,
// so there is no second rendering that can drift from the first.

interface OnboardingIntakeProps {
  studentName?: string
  studentEmail?: string
  markdown?: string
}

const OnboardingIntakeEmail = ({
  studentName = 'A student',
  studentEmail = '',
  markdown = '',
}: OnboardingIntakeProps) => (
  <Html lang="en" dir="ltr">
    <Head />
    <Preview>{`${studentName} finished onboarding`}</Preview>
    <Body style={main}>
      <Container style={container}>
        <Heading style={h1}>{studentName} finished onboarding</Heading>
        <Text style={text}>{studentEmail}</Text>
        <pre style={pre}>{markdown}</pre>
      </Container>
    </Body>
  </Html>
)

const main = { backgroundColor: '#ffffff', fontFamily: 'ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif' }
const container = { margin: '0 auto', padding: '24px', maxWidth: '640px' }
const h1 = { fontSize: '20px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#111111', margin: '0 0 4px' }
const text = { fontSize: '13px', color: '#666666', margin: '0 0 16px' }
const pre = {
  fontFamily: 'ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, monospace',
  fontSize: '12px',
  lineHeight: '1.6',
  color: '#111111',
  whiteSpace: 'pre-wrap' as const,
  wordBreak: 'break-word' as const,
  border: '1px solid #e5e5e5',
  padding: '16px',
  margin: 0,
}

export const template: TemplateEntry = {
  component: OnboardingIntakeEmail,
  // A fixed, greppable subject: the filing skill finds these by subject alone.
  subject: (d: Record<string, any>) => `[LSATU ONBOARDING] ${d.studentName ?? 'Unknown student'}`,
  to: 'contact@aspiringattorneys.com',
  displayName: 'Onboarding intake (to Joshua)',
  previewData: {
    studentName: 'Sofia Minassian',
    studentEmail: 'sofia@example.com',
    markdown: '---\nstudent: Sofia Minassian\n---\n\n# Onboarding intake - Sofia Minassian\n',
  },
}
  const profile: OnboardingProfile = {
    class_id: classId,
    display_name: (row.display_name as string) ?? null,
    email: authUser?.user?.email ?? null,
    onboarding: (row.onboarding as Record<string, unknown>) ?? null,
    onboarding_completed_at: row.onboarding_completed_at as string,
  };

  const markdown = buildOnboardingMarkdown(profile);
  await sendAppEmail("onboarding-intake", "", {
    // Same key for the same student forever, so a retry cannot double-send.
    idempotencyKey: `onboarding-intake:${classId}`,
    replyTo: profile.email ?? undefined,
    templateData: {
      studentName: profile.display_name || profile.email || "Unknown student",
      studentEmail: profile.email ?? "",
      markdown,
    },
  });

  // Stamped only after a send that did not throw. A failed send leaves the
  // profile owed, and the next sweep retries it.
  const { error } = await admin
    .from("profiles")
    .update({ onboarding_notified_at: new Date().toISOString() })
    .eq("class_id", classId);

## assistant

I merged [PR #145](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/pull/145) with the email alert included (commit `786c425`), and confirmed both are on `main`. None of it is live yet: the webhook needs the Lovable deploy below. The typecheck passed and the webhook code compiles.

**What happens on every new paid charge once it's deployed.** This covers payment links, the native single session and Cal.com bookings:
1. It lands in the Payments list on Today within seconds, and the list updates by itself.
2. It sends one notification to your admin accounts, which shows up live.
3. It sends one email to contact@aspiringattorneys.com with the subject `[LSATU PAYMENT] Name paid $X`. The email says:
   - who paid and their email
   - the amount and the time (Eastern)
   - what it was for, such as "10 hour reduced (package, 10 passes)"
   - what's waiting on you: grant the passes, link a student, or nothing

   There's also an "Open Today" button, and hitting Reply goes to the payer.
4. A Stripe retry can't send you a second email, because each email is tied to its charge.
5. If the email fails, the payment is still recorded and the failure is logged, so it can't hold up the payment or make Stripe retry.

**Your step:** paste this into Lovable. It spends credits:

```
Deploy the Supabase Edge Function booking-commerce-webhook exactly as it is on main. Do not edit any code, schema, or other files. Reply only with the deploy result.
```

**After Lovable confirms:**
1. I publish the frontend (free) for the live-updating Payments panel.
2. You pay the $1 Tester link once.
3. I check that the ledger row, the notification and the email all landed, and how many seconds each took.

I logged this in `wiki/log.md`.
